Returns the entity that entity hit in collision number index, from 1 to CountCollisions(entity). The list is renewed by each UpdateWorld, and an index out of range gives an error.

```basic
; Find out what the player ran into
Graphics3D 800, 600
Collisions 1, 2, 2, 2
player = CreateSphere()
EntityType player, 1
EntityRadius player, 1
UpdateWorld
For i = 1 To CountCollisions(player)
    Print "Hit entity " + CollisionEntity(player, i)
Next
```

## Picking

### EntityPickMode

`EntityPickMode entity%, pick_geometry%, [obscurer%=1]`

Makes entity pickable: pick_geometry 0 none, 1 sphere, 2 polygon, 3 box. With obscurer 1 it also blocks EntityVisible checks.

```basic
; Make the crate pickable by its polygons (2) and let it block sight lines
Graphics3D 800, 600
crate = CreateCube()
EntityPickMode crate, 2, 1
```

### LinePick

`LinePick(x#, y#, z#, dx#, dy#, dz#, [radius#=0])` → integer

Picks along a line from x,y,z in direction and length dx,dy,dz, optionally with a thickness of radius. Returns the picked entity, or 0.

```basic
; Cast a ray from the origin straight along +Z for 100 units
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
hit = LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100)
If hit <> 0 Then Print "Hit at z = " + PickedZ()
```

### EntityPick

`EntityPick(entity%, range#)` → integer

Picks from entity straight ahead up to range units. Returns the picked entity, or 0.

```basic
; Shoot straight ahead from the player, up to 50 units
Graphics3D 800, 600
player = CreateCube()
target = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity target, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode target, 1
hit = EntityPick(player, 50)
If hit = target Then Print "Target in the sights"
```

### CameraPick

`CameraPick(camera%, viewport_x#, viewport_y#)` → integer

Picks the entity under screen point viewport_x,viewport_y of camera. Returns the picked entity, or 0.

```basic
; Which object is under the mouse?
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
cube = CreateCube()
PositionEntity cube, 0, 0, 5
EntityPickMode cube, 2
picked = CameraPick(camera, MouseX(), MouseY())
If picked <> 0 Then Print "Mouse is over entity " + picked
```

### PickedX

`PickedX()` → float

Returns the world x of the point hit by the last LinePick, EntityPick or CameraPick. It is 0 when that pick hit nothing, so check the value the pick returned before using it.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Hit at x = " + PickedX()
```

### PickedY

`PickedY()` → float

Returns the world y of the point hit by the last LinePick, EntityPick or CameraPick. It is 0 when that pick hit nothing, so check the value the pick returned before using it.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Hit at y = " + PickedY()
```

### PickedZ

`PickedZ()` → float

Returns the world z of the point hit by the last LinePick, EntityPick or CameraPick. It is 0 when that pick hit nothing, so check the value the pick returned before using it.

```basic
; Place a marker where the ray hit
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
marker = CreateSphere()
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then PositionEntity marker, PickedX(), PickedY(), PickedZ()
```

### PickedNX

`PickedNX()` → float

Returns the X component of the surface normal at the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Normal x = " + PickedNX()
```

### PickedNY

`PickedNY()` → float

Returns the Y component of the surface normal at the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Normal y = " + PickedNY()
```

### PickedNZ

`PickedNZ()` → float

Returns the Z component of the surface normal at the last pick.

```basic
; A wall facing us has a normal pointing back along -Z
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Normal z = " + PickedNZ()
```

### PickedTime

`PickedTime()` → float

Returns the time (0 to 1 along the line) of the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Hit " + PickedTime() + " of the way along the line"   ; 0 to 1
```

### PickedEntity

`PickedEntity()` → integer

Returns the entity hit by the last pick, or 0.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
LinePick 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100
If PickedEntity() = wall Then Print "Hit the wall"
```

### PickedTriangle

`PickedTriangle()` → integer

Returns the index of the triangle hit by the last pick.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then Print "Triangle " + PickedTriangle()
```

### PickedSurface

`PickedSurface()` → integer

Returns 1 if the last pick hit a triangle (an entity with EntityPickMode 2), otherwise 0. Unlike Blitz3D it is not a surface handle, so do not pass it to surface commands; use PickedEntity and PickedTriangle.

```basic
; PickedSurface says whether the last pick hit a triangle
Graphics3D 800, 600
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2
If LinePick(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 100) Then
    Print "Triangle hit: " + PickedSurface()   ; 1
    Print "Which triangle: " + PickedTriangle()
EndIf
```

### CameraProject

`CameraProject camera%, x#, y#, z#`

Projects world point x,y,z through camera to the screen; read the result with ProjectedX/Y/Z.

```basic
; Find where a 3D point lands on the screen, e.g. for a health bar above an enemy
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
enemy = CreateCube()
PositionEntity enemy, 2, 1, 10
CameraProject camera, EntityX(enemy), EntityY(enemy) + 2, EntityZ(enemy)
Print "Screen position: " + ProjectedX() + ", " + ProjectedY()
```

### ProjectedX

`ProjectedX()` → float

Returns the screen x, in pixels, of the point given to the last CameraProject. It is 0 if that point was behind the camera, and can lie outside the window if the point is off to the side.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraProject camera, 0, 0, 10
Print "Screen x = " + ProjectedX()
```

### ProjectedY

`ProjectedY()` → float

Returns the screen y, in pixels from the top, of the point given to the last CameraProject. It is 0 if that point was behind the camera, and can lie outside the window if the point is above or below.

```basic
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraProject camera, 0, 0, 10
Print "Screen y = " + ProjectedY()
```

### ProjectedZ

`ProjectedZ()` → float

Returns the depth from the last CameraProject; 0 means the point is behind the camera.

```basic
; 0 means the point is behind the camera
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
CameraProject camera, 0, 0, -10
If ProjectedZ() = 0 Then Print "Behind the camera" Else Print "In front"
```

### EntityInView

`EntityInView(entity%, camera%)` → integer

Returns 1 if the origin (centre point) of entity lies inside the window as seen by camera, and 0 if off-screen or behind it. Only the origin is tested, so a large object with its origin out of view counts as not visible.

```basic
; Only draw an enemy's health bar when it is on screen
Graphics3D 800, 600
camera = CreateCamera()
enemy = CreateCube()
PositionEntity enemy, 0, 0, 10
If EntityInView(enemy, camera) Then Print "Enemy visible"
```

### EntityVisible

`EntityVisible(src_entity%, dest_entity%)` → integer

Returns 1 if nothing pickable as an obscurer blocks the line between src_entity and dest_entity.

```basic
; Can the guard see the player, or is a wall in the way?
Graphics3D 800, 600
guard = CreateCube()
player = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity player, 0, 0, 20
wall = CreateCube()
PositionEntity wall, 0, 0, 10
EntityPickMode wall, 2, 1   ; obscurer = 1
If EntityVisible(guard, player) Then Print "Spotted!" Else Print "Hidden"
```

## Debug drawing

### DebugLine

`DebugLine x1#, y1#, z1#, x2#, y2#, z2#, [red#=255], [green#=255], [blue#=255]`

**(ext)** Draws a line from x1, y1, z1 to x2, y2, z2 in the 3D world for the next frame, in colour red, green, blue (default white). Call it every frame for as long as you want to see it.

```basic
; show where a ray goes: from the player 20 units forward, in red
player = CreatePivot()
TFormPoint 0, 0, 20, player, 0
DebugLine EntityX(player), EntityY(player), EntityZ(player), TFormedX(), TFormedY(), TFormedZ(), 255, 0, 0
```

### DebugBox

`DebugBox x#, y#, z#, width#, height#, depth#, [red#=255], [green#=255], [blue#=255]`

**(ext)** Draws the edges of a box centred on x, y, z, width by height by depth, for the next frame (default white).

```basic
; show the area a trigger covers, in green, every frame
Graphics3D 800, 600
cam = CreateCamera() : PositionEntity cam, 0, 3, -12
While Not KeyHit(1)
    DebugBox 0, 1, 0, 4, 2, 4, 0, 255, 0
    RenderWorld : Flip
Wend
```

### DebugSphere

`DebugSphere x#, y#, z#, radius#, [red#=255], [green#=255], [blue#=255]`

**(ext)** Draws a wire sphere of radius around x, y, z for the next frame (default white).

```basic
; show how far an enemy can hear: an orange sphere of radius 15 that follows it
Graphics3D 800, 600
cam = CreateCamera() : PositionEntity cam, 0, 5, -40
enemy = CreateCube()
While Not KeyHit(1)
    TurnEntity enemy, 0, 1, 0 : MoveEntity enemy, 0, 0, 0.1
    DebugSphere EntityX(enemy), EntityY(enemy), EntityZ(enemy), 15, 255, 160, 0
    RenderWorld : Flip
Wend
```

### DebugEntity

`DebugEntity entity%, [red#=255], [green#=255], [blue#=0]`

**(ext)** Draws entity's bounding box, turned and scaled with it, for the next frame (default yellow).

```basic
; outline the crate in yellow while it spins, to see its bounding box
Graphics3D 800, 600
cam = CreateCamera()
crate = CreateCube() : MoveEntity crate, 0, 0, 5
While Not KeyHit(1)
    TurnEntity crate, 0, 1, 0
    DebugEntity crate
    RenderWorld : Flip
Wend
```

### DebugAxes

`DebugAxes entity%, [size#=1]`

**(ext)** Draws entity's own X (red), Y (green) and Z (blue) axes, size long, for the next frame: shows which way it faces.

```basic
; see which way a turned ship faces (blue is forward)
ship = CreateCone()
TurnEntity ship, 0, 45, 0
DebugAxes ship, 3
```

### DebugNavMesh

`DebugNavMesh navmesh%, [red#=0], [green#=255], [blue#=120]`

**(ext)** Draws the triangles of a navigation mesh for the next frame (default green), to see where agents can walk.

```basic
; see where agents can walk
floor = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity floor, 10, 0.1, 10
navmesh = CreateNavMesh(floor)
DebugNavMesh navmesh
```

### DebugPhysics

`DebugPhysics enable%`

**(ext)** Turns drawing of every physics collider (3D and 2D) on (enable 1) or off (0), every frame until turned off.

```basic
; show the colliders of a falling ball and the floor, until turned off
Graphics3D 800, 600
cam = CreateCamera() : PositionEntity cam, 0, 3, -10
floor = CreateCube() : ScaleEntity floor, 8, 0.5, 8 : EntityPhysics floor, 2, 1
ball = CreateSphere() : PositionEntity ball, 0, 5, 0 : EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2, 1
DebugPhysics True
While Not KeyHit(1)
    UpdateWorld : RenderWorld : Flip
Wend
```

### DebugCollisions

`DebugCollisions enable%`

**(ext)** Turns drawing of every entity's collision shape on (1) or off (0): EntityRadius spheres and ellipsoids in yellow, EntityBox boxes in cyan, every frame until turned off.

```basic
; show the collision sphere of the player and the box of a wall, until turned off
Graphics3D 800, 600
cam = CreateCamera() : PositionEntity cam, 0, 3, -10
player = CreateSphere() : EntityType player, 1 : EntityRadius player, 1
wall = CreateCube() : MoveEntity wall, 4, 0, 0 : EntityType wall, 2 : EntityBox wall, -1, -1, -1, 2, 2, 2
DebugCollisions True
While Not KeyHit(1)
    RenderWorld : Flip
Wend
```

## Cloth and ropes

### CreateCloth

`CreateCloth(width#, height#, [columns%=16], [rows%=16])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a sheet of cloth width by height, with columns by rows squares, and returns it. It hangs down from its top edge in the entity's XY plane: position and turn it, then pin it (ClothPinEdge) or let it fall. Drawn on both sides.

```basic
; a banner hanging from a beam
banner = CreateCloth(2, 3, 16, 24)
PositionEntity banner, 0, 5, 0
ClothPinEdge banner, 0
```

### CreateRope

`CreateRope(x1#, y1#, z1#, x2#, y2#, z2#, [segments%=20], [thickness#=0.05], [length#=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a rope from x1, y1, z1 to x2, y2, z2 in segments pieces, thickness across, and returns it. length 0 makes it exactly as long as the distance; a longer length lets it sag.

```basic
; a rope from a hook 5 units up to the ground, 20 pieces, with a little slack
Graphics3D 800, 600
rope = CreateRope(0, 5, 0, 1, 0, 0, 20, 0.05, 6)
ClothPin rope, 0
```

### ClothPin

`ClothPin cloth%, column%, [row%=0], [entity%=0]`

**(ext)** Holds one point of a cloth (column, row; row 0 is the top) or rope (column is the point, 0 the start, -1 the end) still. With entity, the point is held to that entity and moves with it.

```basic
; a washing-line towel held by two pegs
towel = CreateCloth(1, 1.4, 10, 14)
ClothPin towel, 0, 0
ClothPin towel, 10, 0
```

### ClothUnpin

`ClothUnpin cloth%, column%, [row%=0]`

**(ext)** Lets a pinned point go again: a flag torn from its pole, a rope cut loose.

```basic
; one peg lets go and the towel swings down
towel = CreateCloth(1, 1.4, 10, 14)
ClothPin towel, 0, 0
ClothPin towel, 10, 0
ClothUnpin towel, 10, 0
```

### ClothPinEdge

`ClothPinEdge cloth%, [edge%=0], [entity%=0]`

**(ext)** Pins a whole edge of a cloth: edge 0 top, 1 bottom, 2 left, 3 right; with entity, it moves with that entity (a cape on a character, a curtain on a moving rail).

```basic
; a cape that follows its wearer
hero = CreateCylinder()
cape = CreateCloth(0.9, 1.5, 8, 12)
PositionEntity cape, 0, 0.9, -0.4
ClothPinEdge cape, 0, hero
```

### ClothStiffness

`ClothStiffness cloth%, [stretch#=1], [bend#=0.15]`

**(ext)** Sets how much a cloth or rope resists stretching (stretch, 0 to 1, default 1) and folding (bend, 0 to 1, default 0.15): low bend for silk, high for canvas.

```basic
; stiff canvas that hardly folds
Graphics3D 800, 600
tent = CreateCloth(3, 2)
ClothStiffness tent, 1, 0.8
```

### ClothDamping

`ClothDamping cloth%, damping#`

**(ext)** Sets how quickly a cloth or rope stops moving (0 to 1, default 0.01): more for heavy wet cloth, less for light fabric.

```basic
; heavy wet cloth that settles fast
Graphics3D 800, 600
sheet = CreateCloth(2, 2)
ClothDamping sheet, 0.1
```

### ClothGravity

`ClothGravity cloth%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Sets the pull on a cloth or rope in units a second squared (default 0, -9.8, 0).

```basic
; under water, things sink slowly
weed = CreateRope(0, 0, 0, 0, 2, 0, 12, 0.03)
ClothGravity weed, 0, 1.5, 0          ; it floats upwards instead
```

### ClothWind

`ClothWind cloth%, amount#`

**(ext)** Sets how much the wind (SetWindDirection and SetWindStrength) blows the cloth or rope, with gusts (default 1); 0 means not at all.

```basic
flag = CreateCloth(1.2, 2)
ClothPinEdge flag, 0
SetWindDirection 1, 0
SetWindStrength 0.3
ClothWind flag, 3
```

### ClothCollide

`ClothCollide cloth%, entity%, [radius#=0]`

**(ext)** Makes a cloth or rope slide over entity as a sphere of radius (0 uses its EntityRadius, or 1). Call once for each body, ball or pole it should touch.

```basic
; a tablecloth over a round table and a ball on it
table = CreateCylinder(24)
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityRadius ball, 0.5
cloth = CreateCloth(3, 3)
ClothCollide cloth, table, 1
ClothCollide cloth, ball
```

### ClothFloor

`ClothFloor cloth%, y#, [enable%=1]`

**(ext)** Stops a cloth or rope falling below height y (enable 1), or lets it fall through (0).

```basic
sheet = CreateCloth(2, 2)
ClothFloor sheet, 0                   ; lies on the ground at height 0
```

### ClothTear

`ClothTear cloth%, stretch#`

**(ext)** Lets a cloth tear where it is stretched past stretch times its length (1.5 tears easily, 3 needs a hard pull); 1 or less never tears (the default).

```basic
; a sail that rips in a storm
sail = CreateCloth(2, 3)
ClothPinEdge sail, 0
ClothTear sail, 1.6
```

### ResetCloth

`ResetCloth cloth%`

**(ext)** Puts a cloth or rope back as it was made, at the entity's current place, still.

```basic
flag = CreateCloth(1.2, 2)
ClothPinEdge flag, 0
ResetCloth flag                       ; straight and still again
```

### ClothPointX

`ClothPointX(cloth%, column%, [row%=0])` → float

**(ext)** Returns the world X of a point of a cloth (column, row) or rope (column, -1 the end): to hang a lantern on a rope or attach something to a corner.

```basic
; a lantern hangs on the end of a rope
rope = CreateRope(0, 4, 0, 0, 2, 0)
ClothPin rope, 0
lantern = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity lantern, ClothPointX(rope, -1), ClothPointY(rope, -1), ClothPointZ(rope, -1)
```

### ClothPointY

`ClothPointY(cloth%, column%, [row%=0])` → float

**(ext)** Returns the world Y of a point of a cloth or rope.

```basic
sheet = CreateCloth(2, 2)
Print ClothPointY(sheet, 8, 16)       ; how low the bottom middle hangs
```

### ClothPointZ

`ClothPointZ(cloth%, column%, [row%=0])` → float

**(ext)** Returns the world Z of a point of a cloth or rope.

```basic
rope = CreateRope(0, 4, 0, 0, 2, 0)
Print ClothPointZ(rope, -1)
```

## Character controllers

### CreateCharController

`CreateCharController(entity%, [radius#=0.4], [height#=1.8])` → integer

**(ext)** Makes entity a walking character whose feet are at its origin, with a capsule of radius and height, and returns the character. The world must be solid to physics (EntityPhysics). See docs/CHARACTERS.md.

```basic
; a hero whose entity origin is at its feet, walking on a static floor
Graphics3D 800, 600
floor = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity floor, 20, 0.5, 20
PositionEntity floor, 0, -0.5, 0
EntityPhysics floor, 2                ; body_type 2 = static: solid, never moves
hero = CreateCube()
PositionEntity hero, 0, 1, 0
player = CreateCharController(hero, 0.4, 1.8)   ; radius 0.4, height 1.8
cam = CreateCamera()
PositionEntity cam, 0, 3, -8
While Not KeyHit(KEY_ESCAPE)
    CharMove player, KeyDown(KEY_D) - KeyDown(KEY_A), 0, KeyDown(KEY_W) - KeyDown(KEY_S)
    UpdateChar player
    UpdateWorld
    RenderWorld
    Flip
Wend
```

### CreateCharController2D

`CreateCharController2D(x#, y#, [width#=24], [height#=32], [shape%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Makes a 2D character with its feet at x, y and returns it. shape is CHAR_BOX (the default) or CHAR_CAPSULE.

```basic
; a platformer hero: a 26 by 44 box with its feet at 100, 400, falling onto the floor
Graphics 960, 540
floor = CreateBody2D(1, 480, 520)     ; body type 1 = static
Body2DBox floor, 960, 40
player = CreateCharController2D(100, 400, 26, 44)
While Not KeyHit(KEY_ESCAPE)
    CharMove player, KeyDown(KEY_RIGHT) - KeyDown(KEY_LEFT)
    UpdateChar player
    Cls
    Rect CharX(player) - 13, CharY(player) - CharHeight(player), 26, CharHeight(player)
    Flip
Wend
```

### FreeCharController

`FreeCharController character%`

**(ext)** Frees a character. A 3D character's entity stays where it is.

```basic
; the entity stays, but no longer walks by itself
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
FreeCharController player
```

### UpdateChar

`UpdateChar character%, [seconds#=0]`

**(ext)** Moves character for one frame after its input has been given. seconds is the time to advance; 0 measures the time since the last update.

```basic
; once a frame, after giving it input
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
UpdateChar player
```

### CharMove

`CharMove character%, x#, [y#=0], [z#=0]`

**(ext)** Sets which way character wants to go in world directions, up to 1 long. y only counts when flying. In 2D use x and, for ladders and top-down games, y.

```basic
; run along +X
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
CharMove player, 1, 0, 0
```

### CharWalk

`CharWalk character%, ahead#, side#, yaw#`

**(ext)** Sets where a 3D character wants to go relative to the direction yaw (usually the camera's): ahead and side from -1 to 1.

```basic
; W means away from the camera, whichever way it faces
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
camera = CreateCamera()
CharWalk player, KeyDown(KEY_W) - KeyDown(KEY_S), KeyDown(KEY_D) - KeyDown(KEY_A), EntityYaw(camera)
```

### CharJump

`CharJump character%`

**(ext)** Asks character to jump. Call it when the button is pressed; a press just before landing still counts.

```basic
; when the button is pressed
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If KeyHit(KEY_SPACE) Then CharJump player
```

### CharHoldJump

`CharHoldJump character%, held%`

**(ext)** Tells character whether the jump button is held. Letting go early shortens the jump. Without this every jump is full height.

```basic
; tap for a hop, hold for a leap
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
CharHoldJump player, KeyDown(KEY_SPACE)
```

### CharSprint

`CharSprint character%, enable%`

**(ext)** Turns sprinting on (enable 1) or off (0) for character. While on, its walking speed is multiplied by the sprint setting (1.6 in 3D, 1.5 in 2D by default). It stays set until you change it, and crouching overrides it.

```basic
; run faster while Shift is down
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
CharSprint player, KeyDown(KEY_LSHIFT)
```

### CharCrouch

`CharCrouch character%, enable%`

**(ext)** Asks character to crouch (enable 1) or stand (0). It stays down until there is room to stand.

```basic
; hold Ctrl to get under low things
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
CharCrouch player, KeyDown(KEY_LCONTROL)
```

### CharDash

`CharDash character%, x#, y#, z#, speed#, seconds#`

**(ext)** Sends character in direction x, y, z at speed for seconds, ignoring gravity.

```basic
; a quick burst forward
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
CharDash player, 0, 0, 1, 16, 0.2
```

### CharImpulse

`CharImpulse character%, x#, [y#=0], [z#=0]`

**(ext)** Shoves character by a velocity that fades away; an upward part lifts it off the ground.

```basic
; knocked back by an explosion
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
CharImpulse player, 8, 4, 0
```

### CharDrop

`CharDrop character%`

**(ext)** Lets character fall through one-way platforms (EntityOneWay, Body2DOneWay) for a quarter of a second and makes it leave the ground it stands on at once. Call it on the frame the player presses down and jump.

```basic
; a thin platform the player can jump onto, and drop through with Down + Space
Graphics 960, 540
ground = CreateBody2D(1, 480, 520)
Body2DBox ground, 960, 40
ledge = CreateBody2D(1, 480, 400)
Body2DBox ledge, 200, 12
Body2DOneWay ledge                    ; solid from above, passable from below
player = CreateCharController2D(480, 300)
While Not KeyHit(KEY_ESCAPE)
    If KeyHit(KEY_SPACE)
        If KeyDown(KEY_DOWN) Then CharDrop player Else CharJump player
    EndIf
    UpdateChar player
    Cls
    Rect 380, 394, 200, 12
    Rect CharX(player) - 12, CharY(player) - 32, 24, 32
    Flip
Wend
```

### CharMode

`CharMode character%, mode%`

**(ext)** Sets how character moves: CHAR_WALK, CHAR_FLY (no gravity, all directions, still solid) or CHAR_NOCLIP (through everything).

```basic
; a top-down game: no gravity, so CharMove can go in any direction, and walls still stop it
Graphics 960, 540
player = CreateCharController2D(480, 270, 24, 24)
CharMode player, CHAR_FLY
While Not KeyHit(KEY_ESCAPE)
    CharMove player, KeyDown(KEY_RIGHT) - KeyDown(KEY_LEFT), KeyDown(KEY_DOWN) - KeyDown(KEY_UP)
    UpdateChar player
    Cls
    Rect CharX(player) - 12, CharY(player) - 24, 24, 24
    Flip
Wend
```

### CharSet

`CharSet character%, setting$, value#`

**(ext)** Changes a setting of character by name, such as "speed" or "jump_speed". The settings are listed in docs/CHARACTERS.md.

```basic
; a double jump and a higher leap
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
CharSet player, "air_jumps", 1
CharSet player, "jump_speed", 10
```

### CharGet

`CharGet(character%, setting$)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the tuning setting called setting of character as a decimal, for example "speed", "gravity" or "jump_speed". Names ignore case and an unknown name is a runtime error. Values are in metres (3D) or pixels (2D); CharSet changes them.

```basic
; read a setting back
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharGet(player, "speed")
```

### PositionChar

`PositionChar character%, x#, y#, [z#=0]`

**(ext)** Moves character at once so its feet are at x, y, z (in 2D only x and y count, in pixels), without checking for obstacles on the way. Its velocity is kept, so call SetCharVelocity with zeros for a clean respawn.

```basic
; back to the last checkpoint
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
PositionChar player, 0, 2, 0
```

### SetCharVelocity

`SetCharVelocity character%, x#, y#, [z#=0]`

**(ext)** Replaces the velocity of character with x, y, z, in metres per second (3D) or pixels per second (2D). Gravity and steering keep acting afterwards. In 3D +y is up; in 2D +y points down the screen.

```basic
; a launch pad
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
SetCharVelocity player, 0, 18, 0
```

### CharX

`CharX(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the x position of the middle of character's feet as of the last UpdateChar: the world x in 3D, pixels in 2D.

```basic
; where its feet are
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharX(player)
```

### CharY

`CharY(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the y position of character's feet as of the last UpdateChar. In 3D it is the height of the ground it stands on; in 2D it is pixels down the screen, so its head is at CharY minus CharHeight.

```basic
; fell out of the level
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharY(player) < -20 Then PositionChar player, 0, 2, 0
```

### CharZ

`CharZ(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the z (depth) position of character's feet as of the last UpdateChar. In 2D it is always 0.

```basic
; where its feet are
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharZ(player)
```

### CharVX

`CharVX(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the velocity of character along x, in metres per second (3D) or pixels per second (2D), as of the last UpdateChar. Positive means towards +x.

```basic
; how fast it moves across the ground
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
pace# = Sqr(CharVX(player) * CharVX(player) + CharVZ(player) * CharVZ(player))
```

### CharVY

`CharVY(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the velocity of character along y as of the last UpdateChar. In 3D positive is up, so falling is negative; in 2D y points down the screen, so a rising character has a negative value.

```basic
; rising or falling
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharVY(player) < 0 Then Print "falling"
```

### CharVZ

`CharVZ(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the velocity of character along z, in metres per second, as of the last UpdateChar. Positive means towards +z. In 2D it is always 0.

```basic
; its speed along z
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharVZ(player)
```

### CharHeight

`CharHeight(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the current height of character in metres (3D) or pixels (2D): the full height standing, lower while crouching, and back to full only once there is room to stand up.

```basic
; draw a 2D character at its current height: it gets shorter while Down is held
Graphics 960, 540
floor = CreateBody2D(1, 480, 520)
Body2DBox floor, 960, 40
player = CreateCharController2D(100, 400, 26, 44)
While Not KeyHit(KEY_ESCAPE)
    CharCrouch player, KeyDown(KEY_DOWN)
    UpdateChar player
    Cls
    Rect CharX(player) - 13, CharY(player) - CharHeight(player), 26, CharHeight(player)
    Flip
Wend
```

### CharGrounded

`CharGrounded(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if character stood on ground it can walk on (not steeper than the max_slope setting) at the last UpdateChar, otherwise 0. It is 0 while jumping or falling.

```basic
; only let it attack on the ground
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharGrounded(player) Then Print "on the ground"
```

### CharLanded

`CharLanded(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if character touched down after being in the air during the last UpdateChar, otherwise 0. It is true for one update only, so check it every frame to play landing sounds and dust.

```basic
; a puff of dust on landing
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharLanded(player) Then Print "thump"
```

### CharLeftGround

`CharLeftGround(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if character walked off an edge during the last update.

```basic
; walked off an edge
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharLeftGround(player) Then Print "whoa"
```

### CharJumped

`CharJumped(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if a jump (ground, air or wall jump) really started during the last UpdateChar, otherwise 0. CharJump only asks for a jump and may be refused in the air, so use this to play the sound.

```basic
; the sound plays only for a jump that really happened
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharJumped(player) Then Print "boing"
```

### CharWallJumped

`CharWallJumped(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if character jumped off a wall during the last update.

```basic
; a different sound for a wall jump
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharWallJumped(player) Then Print "kick"
```

### CharStepped

`CharStepped(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if character stepped up a stair during the last update.

```basic
; a footstep on each stair
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharStepped(player) Then Print "step"
```

### CharImpact

`CharImpact(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns how fast character was falling when it last landed, during the last update.

```basic
; fall damage
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
health = 100
If CharLanded(player) And CharImpact(player) > 15 Then health = health - 20
```

### CharOnWall

`CharOnWall(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if character touched a wall beside it during the last UpdateChar, otherwise 0. Wall sliding and wall jumping (settings wall_slide and wall_jump) depend on it; CharWallNX, CharWallNY and CharWallNZ give the wall's direction.

```basic
; against a wall
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharOnWall(player) Then Print "wall"
```

### CharWallSliding

`CharWallSliding(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if character is in the air, pushing into a wall and falling slowly down it, otherwise 0. It needs the wall_slide setting above 0 (off by default), which is the sliding speed.

```basic
; sparks while sliding down
player = CreateCharController2D(100, 400)
If CharWallSliding(player) Then Print "sliding"
```

### CharWallNX

`CharWallNX(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the x part of the direction the wall character last touched faces.

```basic
; which way the wall faces
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharWallNX(player)
```

### CharWallNY

`CharWallNY(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the y part of the direction the wall character last touched faces.

```basic
; which way the wall faces
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharWallNY(player)
```

### CharWallNZ

`CharWallNZ(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the z part of the direction the wall character last touched faces.

```basic
; which way the wall faces
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharWallNZ(player)
```

### CharOnCeiling

`CharOnCeiling(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if character bumped its head during the last update.

```basic
; bumped its head
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharOnCeiling(player) Then Print "ouch"
```

### CharSliding

`CharSliding(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if character is on a slope too steep to stand on.

```basic
; on a slope too steep to stand on
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharSliding(player) Then Print "slipping"
```

### CharCrouching

`CharCrouching(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 while character is crouching, otherwise 0. After CharCrouch is turned off it stays 1 until there is room above its head to stand up.

```basic
; a crouching player is harder to spot
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
If CharCrouching(player) Then Print "hidden"
```

### CharDashing

`CharDashing(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 while a dash started with CharDash is still going, otherwise 0. Use it to make the character immune or to draw a trail.

```basic
; can't be hurt while dashing
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
safe = CharDashing(player)
```

### CharGroundObject

`CharGroundObject(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the entity or 2D body under character, or 0.

```basic
; standing on a certain thing
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
pad = CreateCube()
If CharGroundObject(player) = pad Then Print "on the pad"
```

### CharGroundNX

`CharGroundNX(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the x part of the direction the ground under character faces.

```basic
; how the ground leans
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharGroundNX(player)
```

### CharGroundNY

`CharGroundNY(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the y part of the direction the ground under character faces.

```basic
; 1 is flat ground, less is a slope
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharGroundNY(player)
```

### CharGroundNZ

`CharGroundNZ(character%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the z part of the direction the ground under character faces.

```basic
; how the ground leans
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharGroundNZ(player)
```

### CharAirJumpsLeft

`CharAirJumpsLeft(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many more jumps in mid-air character can make. It refills to the air_jumps setting (0 by default, so 1 means a double jump) whenever it stands on the ground, and drops by one for each air jump.

```basic
; show how many jumps remain
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharAirJumpsLeft(player)
```

### CharFacing

`CharFacing(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns -1 or 1, the way character last moved along x.

```basic
; flip a sprite the way it last moved
image = CreateImage(26, 44)
player = CreateCharController2D(100, 400)
DrawImageEx image, CharX(player), CharY(player), 0, 0, CharFacing(player), 1
```

### CharState

`CharState(character%)` → string

**(ext)** Returns one word for what character is doing: idle, walk, run, jump, fall, crouch, crawl, slide, wallslide, dash or fly.

```basic
; one word to choose an animation by
hero = CreatePivot()
player = CreateCharController(hero)
Print CharState(player)
```

### CharBody

`CharBody(character%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns a 2D character's body, or 0 for a 3D character.

```basic
; its 2D body, for the body commands
player = CreateCharController2D(100, 400)
Print Body2DX(CharBody(player))
```

### EntityOneWay

`EntityOneWay entity%, [enable%=1]`

**(ext)** Makes a physics entity solid to characters only from above (enable 1) or solid all round (0).

```basic
; a shelf to jump up through
shelf = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics shelf, 2
EntityOneWay shelf
```

### Body2DOneWay

`Body2DOneWay body%, [enable%=1]`

**(ext)** Makes a 2D body solid to characters only from above (enable 1) or solid all round (0). Call it after giving the body its shapes.

```basic
; a ledge to jump up through
ledge = CreateBody2D(1, 300, 400)
Body2DBox ledge, 180, 12
Body2DOneWay ledge
```

## 3D physics

### EntityPhysics

`EntityPhysics entity%, [body_type%=1], [shape%=0], [mass#=1]`

**(ext)** Makes entity a rigid body: body_type 1 dynamic, 2 static, 3 kinematic; shape 0 automatic, 1 box, 2 sphere, 3 capsule, 4 cylinder, 5 mesh, 6 convex hull; mass in kilograms.

```basic
; A dynamic box that falls, and a static floor it lands on
floor = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity floor, 10, 0.5, 10
EntityPhysics floor, 2, 1            ; body_type 2 = static, shape 1 = box
crate = CreateCube()
PositionEntity crate, 0, 5, 0
EntityPhysics crate, 1, 1, 2.5       ; dynamic, box shape, 2.5 kg
```

### PhysicsGravity

`PhysicsGravity x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Sets the world's gravity vector x, y, z, in units per second squared.

```basic
; moon gravity: a ball falls slowly
Graphics3D 800, 600
cam = CreateCamera() : PositionEntity cam, 0, 3, -12
ball = CreateSphere() : PositionEntity ball, 0, 8, 0 : EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2, 1
PhysicsGravity 0, -1.6, 0
While Not KeyHit(1)
    UpdateWorld : RenderWorld : Flip
Wend
```

### EntityMass

`EntityMass entity%, mass#`

**(ext)** Sets the mass of entity's physics body, in kilograms, which decides how hard it is to push or stop. Mass below 0.0001 counts as that. It is an error if entity has no physics (EntityPhysics).

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
EntityMass ball, 10                  ; now weighs 10 kilograms
```

### EntityFriction

`EntityFriction entity%, friction#`

**(ext)** Sets the friction of entity's physics body (0 is slippery).

```basic
floor = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics floor, 2, 1
EntityFriction floor, 0.02           ; almost no friction: like ice
```

### EntityBounce

`EntityBounce entity%, bounce#`

**(ext)** Sets how bouncy entity's physics body is, from 0 (no bounce) to 1.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
EntityBounce ball, 0.8               ; 0 = no bounce, 1 = bounces back fully
```

### EntityDamping

`EntityDamping entity%, linear#, angular#`

**(ext)** Sets the linear and angular damping of entity's physics body, which slows its movement and spin.

```basic
; Make a ball slow down quickly as it rolls and spins
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
EntityDamping ball, 0.5, 0.8         ; linear damping, angular damping
```

### EntityGravityScale

`EntityGravityScale entity%, scale#`

**(ext)** Scales how much gravity affects entity's physics body (0 none, 1 normal).

```basic
balloon = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics balloon, 1, 2
EntityGravityScale balloon, 0.1      ; falls slowly: 0 = none, 1 = normal
```

### LockRotation

`LockRotation entity%, x%, y%, z%`

**(ext)** Stops entity's physics body rotating about each axis whose flag x, y, z is 1.

```basic
; A character body that can turn around Y but must not tip over
player = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics player, 1, 3
LockRotation player, 1, 0, 1         ; 1 = locked about X and Z, 0 = free about Y
```

### ApplyForce

`ApplyForce entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Applies a continuous force x, y, z to entity's physics body.

```basic
; A rocket engine: push upwards a little every frame
rocket = CreateCone()
EntityPhysics rocket, 1, 4, 2
For frame = 1 To 60
    ApplyForce rocket, 0, 40, 0
    UpdateWorld
Next
```

### ApplyImpulse

`ApplyImpulse entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Gives entity's physics body an instant push x, y, z.

```basic
; Kick the ball forward once
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
ApplyImpulse ball, 0, 0, 5
```

### ApplyImpulseAt

`ApplyImpulseAt entity%, x#, y#, z#, point_x#, point_y#, point_z#`

**(ext)** Gives entity's physics body an instant push x, y, z at the world point point_x, point_y, point_z, which can also make it spin.

```basic
; Hit the crate near its top edge, so it tips over as well as moving
crate = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics crate, 1, 1
ApplyImpulseAt crate, 3, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0   ; push x,y,z at the world point 0,1,0
```

### ApplyTorque

`ApplyTorque entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Applies a continuous turning force x, y, z to entity's physics body.

```basic
; Keep turning a wheel about its Z axis
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
For frame = 1 To 60
    ApplyTorque wheel, 0, 0, 5
    UpdateWorld
Next
```

### ApplyTorqueImpulse

`ApplyTorqueImpulse entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Gives entity's physics body an instant spin x, y, z.

```basic
; Give a coin a quick flip
coin = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics coin, 1, 4
ApplyTorqueImpulse coin, 2, 0, 0
```

### SetVelocity

`SetVelocity entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Sets the velocity of entity's physics body, in units per second.

```basic
; Shoot a bullet straight ahead at 30 units per second
bullet = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics bullet, 1, 2
SetVelocity bullet, 0, 0, 30
```

### SetAngularVelocity

`SetAngularVelocity entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Sets how fast entity's physics body spins about the X, Y and Z axes, in radians per second, replacing its current spin. It is an error if entity has no physics (EntityPhysics).

```basic
; Start a wheel spinning
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
SetAngularVelocity wheel, 0, 0, 10
```

### VelocityX

`VelocityX(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the X velocity of entity's physics body, in units per second.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
SetVelocity ball, 4, 0, 0
Print "Sideways speed: " + VelocityX(ball)
```

### VelocityY

`VelocityY(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Y velocity of entity's physics body, in units per second.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
UpdateWorld
If VelocityY(ball) < 0 Then Print "The ball is falling"
```

### VelocityZ

`VelocityZ(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Z velocity of entity's physics body, in units per second.

```basic
car = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics car, 1, 1
SetVelocity car, 0, 0, 12
Print "Forward speed: " + VelocityZ(car)
```

### AngularVelocityX

`AngularVelocityX(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the angular velocity of entity's physics body about the X axis.

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
SetAngularVelocity ball, 3, 0, 0
Print "Spin about X: " + AngularVelocityX(ball)
```

### AngularVelocityY

`AngularVelocityY(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the angular velocity of entity's physics body about the Y axis.

```basic
top = CreateCone()
EntityPhysics top, 1, 4
SetAngularVelocity top, 0, 8, 0
Print "Spin about Y: " + AngularVelocityY(top)
```

### AngularVelocityZ

`AngularVelocityZ(entity%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the angular velocity of entity's physics body about the Z axis.

```basic
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
SetAngularVelocity wheel, 0, 0, 5
Print "Spin about Z: " + AngularVelocityZ(wheel)
```

### EntityTouching

`EntityTouching(entity%, other%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if entity's physics body is touching other, otherwise 0.

```basic
floor = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity floor, 10, 0.5, 10
EntityPhysics floor, 2, 1
ball = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity ball, 0, 2, 0
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
UpdateWorld
If EntityTouching(ball, floor) Then Print "On the ground" Else Print "In the air"
```

### CountContacts

`CountContacts(entity%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many different physics bodies entity is touching at the moment; 0 usually means it is in the air. It is an error if entity has no physics (EntityPhysics).

```basic
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
UpdateWorld
Print "The ball touches " + CountContacts(ball) + " bodies"
```

### ContactEntity

`ContactEntity(entity%, index%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns contact number index (counting from 1) of entity, or 0 if there is none.

```basic
; List everything the ball is touching (numbering starts at 1)
ball = CreateSphere()
EntityPhysics ball, 1, 2
UpdateWorld
For index = 1 To CountContacts(ball)
    other = ContactEntity(ball, index)
    Print "Touching entity " + other
Next
```

### PhysicsRayCast

`PhysicsRayCast(x#, y#, z#, dx#, dy#, dz#)` → integer

**(ext)** Casts a ray from x, y, z along dx, dy, dz against physics bodies and returns the first entity hit, or 0; PickedX and the other picked commands give the hit details.

```basic
; Look straight down from above the world and see what is below
hit = PhysicsRayCast(0, 10, 0, 0, -1, 0)   ; start x,y,z then direction dx,dy,dz
If hit <> 0 Then Print "Ground is at height " + PickedY()
```

## Joints and ragdolls

### CreateBallJoint

`CreateBallJoint(entity1%, entity2%, x#, y#, z#)` → integer

**(ext)** Joins the physics bodies of entity1 and entity2 with a ball joint at world point x, y, z and returns the joint.

```basic
; Hang a weight from a fixed anchor, like a pendulum
anchor = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity anchor, 0, 5, 0
EntityPhysics anchor, 2, 2
weight = CreateSphere()
PositionEntity weight, 2, 5, 0
EntityPhysics weight, 1, 2
joint = CreateBallJoint(anchor, weight, 0, 5, 0)   ; joint point x,y,z
```

### CreateHingeJoint

`CreateHingeJoint(entity1%, entity2%, x#, y#, z#, axis_x#, axis_y#, axis_z#)` → integer

**(ext)** Joins entity1 and entity2 with a hinge at world point x, y, z turning about axis axis_x, axis_y, axis_z, and returns the joint.

```basic
; A door that swings about a vertical axis
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
door = CreateCube()
ScaleEntity door, 1, 2, 0.1
PositionEntity door, 1, 0, 0
EntityPhysics door, 1, 1
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, door, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)   ; point 0,0,0 and axis 0,1,0
```

### CreateFixedJoint

`CreateFixedJoint(entity1%, entity2%)` → integer

**(ext)** Locks the physics bodies of entity1 and entity2 together and returns the joint.

```basic
; Glue a gun onto a vehicle so they move as one
vehicle = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics vehicle, 1, 1
gun = CreateCylinder()
PositionEntity gun, 0, 1, 0
EntityPhysics gun, 1, 4
glue = CreateFixedJoint(vehicle, gun)
```

### FreeJoint

`FreeJoint joint%`

**(ext)** Removes joint so the two bodies are no longer connected and move on their own; the bodies stay. Freeing a joint that is already freed or unknown is silently ignored.

```basic
a = CreateCube()
b = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics a, 1, 1
EntityPhysics b, 1, 1
joint = CreateFixedJoint(a, b)
FreeJoint joint                      ; the two bodies are separate again
```

### CreateSliderJoint

`CreateSliderJoint(entity1%, entity2%, x#, y#, z#, axis_x#, axis_y#, axis_z#)` → integer

**(ext)** Joins entity1 and entity2 with a slider at world point x, y, z that moves along axis axis_x, axis_y, axis_z, and returns the joint.

```basic
; A piston that slides up and down along the Y axis
base = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics base, 2, 1
piston = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics piston, 1, 4
slider = CreateSliderJoint(base, piston, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)
```

### JointMotor

`JointMotor joint%, speed#, [max_force#=1000]`

**(ext)** Drives a hinge or slider joint at speed (degrees or units per second), pushing with at most max_force.

```basic
; Spin a hinge like a motor at 90 degrees per second
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, wheel, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0)
JointMotor hinge, 90, 500            ; speed, strongest push
```

### JointTarget

`JointTarget joint%, target#, [stiffness#=1000], [max_force#=100000]`

**(ext)** Pulls a hinge or slider joint towards target (an angle in degrees or a position in units) like a spring with the given stiffness, pushing with at most max_force.

```basic
; Make a hinge swing to 45 degrees and stay there like a spring
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
arm = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics arm, 1, 1
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, arm, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1)
JointTarget hinge, 45, 1000, 5000    ; target angle, stiffness, strongest push
```

### JointMotorOff

`JointMotorOff joint%`

**(ext)** Turns off the motor (JointMotor) or spring target (JointTarget) of joint, so it moves freely again but still obeys JointLimits. For 2D joints use JointMotorOff2D.

```basic
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
wheel = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics wheel, 1, 4
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, wheel, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0)
JointMotor hinge, 90
JointMotorOff hinge                  ; the wheel turns freely again
```

### JointLimits

`JointLimits joint%, min#, max#, [enable%=1]`

**(ext)** Limits a hinge's angle (degrees) or a slider's position (units) to between min and max; enable 0 removes the limits.

```basic
; A door that opens only between 0 and 100 degrees
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
door = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics door, 1, 1
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, door, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)
JointLimits hinge, 0, 100            ; min, max (enable defaults to 1)
JointLimits hinge, 0, 0, 0           ; enable 0 removes the limits again
```

### JointAngle

`JointAngle(joint%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns how far a hinge joint has turned since it was made, in degrees.

```basic
frame = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics frame, 2, 1
door = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics door, 1, 1
hinge = CreateHingeJoint(frame, door, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)
UpdateWorld
Print "The door has turned " + JointAngle(hinge) + " degrees"
```

### JointPosition

`JointPosition(joint%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns how far a slider joint has moved since it was made, in units.

```basic
base = CreateCube()
EntityPhysics base, 2, 1
piston = CreateCylinder()
EntityPhysics piston, 1, 4
slider = CreateSliderJoint(base, piston, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0)
UpdateWorld
Print "The piston has slid " + JointPosition(slider) + " units"
```

### CreateRagdoll

`CreateRagdoll entity%, [thickness#=1]`

**(ext)** Builds a ragdoll of physics bodies and joints for the skeleton of an animated model; thickness scales the limbs. It starts off, following the animation.

```basic
; Add a ragdoll to an animated character; it follows the animation until switched on
man = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/CesiumMan.glb")
CreateRagdoll man, 1.0               ; thickness scales the limbs
```

### RagdollEnable

`RagdollEnable entity%, [enable%=1]`

**(ext)** Turns entity's ragdoll on (enable 1), so the animation stops and the body goes limp, or off (0), giving the bones back to the animation.

```basic
man = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/CesiumMan.glb")
CreateRagdoll man
RagdollEnable man, 1                 ; 1 = go limp
RagdollEnable man, 0                 ; 0 = back to the animation
```

### RagdollActive

`RagdollActive(entity%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if entity's ragdoll is turned on, otherwise 0.

```basic
man = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/CesiumMan.glb")
CreateRagdoll man
RagdollEnable man
If RagdollActive(man) Then Print "The character is limp"
```

### RagdollImpulse

`RagdollImpulse entity%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Gives every body in entity's ragdoll an instant push x, y, z.

```basic
; Blow the character backwards when it is hit
man = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/CesiumMan.glb")
CreateRagdoll man
RagdollEnable man
RagdollImpulse man, 0, 2, -8
```

### FreeRagdoll

`FreeRagdoll entity%`

**(ext)** Removes the physics bodies and joints that CreateRagdoll made for entity; the model itself stays. It is an error if entity has no ragdoll.

```basic
man = LoadAnimMesh("examples/media/CesiumMan.glb")
CreateRagdoll man
FreeRagdoll man                      ; the model is a plain animated mesh again
```

### CreateHingeJoint2D

`CreateHingeJoint2D(body1%, body2%, x#, y#)` → integer

**(ext)** Joins 2D bodies body1 and body2 with a hinge (pin) at x,y pixels that lets them turn. Returns the joint handle.

```basic
; A swinging pendulum: a fixed pivot and a hanging weight
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
pivot = CreateBody2D(1, 400, 100)    ; type 1 = static
Body2DCircle pivot, 5
weight = CreateBody2D(0, 500, 100)   ; type 0 = dynamic
Body2DCircle weight, 20
joint = CreateHingeJoint2D(pivot, weight, 400, 100)   ; pin at x,y pixels
```

### CreateFixedJoint2D

`CreateFixedJoint2D(body1%, body2%, x#, y#)` → integer

**(ext)** Locks 2D bodies body1 and body2 together at x,y pixels. Returns the joint handle.

```basic
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
cart = CreateBody2D(0, 200, 300)
Body2DBox cart, 80, 30
cannon = CreateBody2D(0, 200, 280)
Body2DBox cannon, 20, 30
glue = CreateFixedJoint2D(cart, cannon, 200, 290)
```

### CreateSliderJoint2D

`CreateSliderJoint2D(body1%, body2%, x#, y#, [angle#=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Joins 2D bodies body1 and body2 with a slider at x,y that lets them slide along a line at angle degrees. Returns the joint handle.

```basic
; A platform that can only slide sideways (along angle 0)
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
rail = CreateBody2D(1, 300, 300)
Body2DBox rail, 20, 20
platform = CreateBody2D(0, 300, 300)
Body2DBox platform, 100, 16
slider = CreateSliderJoint2D(rail, platform, 300, 300, 0)
```

### FreeJoint2D

`FreeJoint2D joint%`

**(ext)** Removes 2D joint so the two bodies are no longer connected and move on their own; the bodies stay. Using a joint number that does not exist is an error.

```basic
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
a = CreateBody2D(0, 100, 100)
Body2DBox a, 20, 20
b = CreateBody2D(0, 130, 100)
Body2DBox b, 20, 20
joint = CreateFixedJoint2D(a, b, 115, 100)
FreeJoint2D joint
```

### JointMotor2D

`JointMotor2D joint%, speed#, [max_force#=1000]`

**(ext)** Drives a 2D hinge or slider joint at speed (degrees or pixels per second), pushing with at most max_force.

```basic
; A wheel driven at 180 degrees per second
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
frame = CreateBody2D(1, 300, 300)
Body2DBox frame, 10, 10
wheel = CreateBody2D(0, 300, 300)
Body2DCircle wheel, 30
hinge = CreateHingeJoint2D(frame, wheel, 300, 300)
JointMotor2D hinge, 180, 5000        ; speed, strongest push
```

### JointTarget2D

`JointTarget2D joint%, target#, [stiffness#=1000], [max_force#=100000]`

**(ext)** Pulls a 2D hinge or slider joint like a spring to target (degrees or pixels) with stiffness, pushing with at most max_force.

```basic
; Spring a lever to 30 degrees
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
frame = CreateBody2D(1, 300, 300)
Body2DBox frame, 10, 10
lever = CreateBody2D(0, 340, 300)
Body2DBox lever, 80, 8
hinge = CreateHingeJoint2D(frame, lever, 300, 300)
JointTarget2D hinge, 30, 1000, 50000   ; target angle, stiffness, strongest push
```

### JointMotorOff2D

`JointMotorOff2D joint%`

**(ext)** Turns off the motor or target of 2D joint joint.

```basic
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
frame = CreateBody2D(1, 300, 300)
Body2DBox frame, 10, 10
wheel = CreateBody2D(0, 300, 300)
Body2DCircle wheel, 30
hinge = CreateHingeJoint2D(frame, wheel, 300, 300)
JointMotor2D hinge, 180
JointMotorOff2D hinge
```

### JointLimits2D

`JointLimits2D joint%, min#, max#, [enable%=1]`

**(ext)** Limits a 2D hinge (degrees) or slider (pixels) joint to between min and max; enable 0 removes the limits.

```basic
; A flipper that can only swing between -30 and 30 degrees
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
frame = CreateBody2D(1, 300, 300)
Body2DBox frame, 10, 10
flipper = CreateBody2D(0, 340, 300)
Body2DBox flipper, 80, 8
hinge = CreateHingeJoint2D(frame, flipper, 300, 300)
JointLimits2D hinge, -30, 30
```

### JointAngle2D

`JointAngle2D(joint%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns how far a 2D hinge joint has turned since it was made, in degrees clockwise.

```basic
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
frame = CreateBody2D(1, 300, 300)
Body2DBox frame, 10, 10
lever = CreateBody2D(0, 340, 300)
Body2DBox lever, 80, 8
hinge = CreateHingeJoint2D(frame, lever, 300, 300)
Print "The lever has turned " + JointAngle2D(hinge) + " degrees clockwise"
```

### JointPosition2D

`JointPosition2D(joint%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns how far a 2D slider joint has slid since it was made, in pixels.

```basic
Physics2DGravity 0, 500
rail = CreateBody2D(1, 300, 300)
Body2DBox rail, 20, 20
platform = CreateBody2D(0, 300, 300)
Body2DBox platform, 100, 16
slider = CreateSliderJoint2D(rail, platform, 300, 300, 0)
Print "The platform has slid " + JointPosition2D(slider) + " pixels"
```

## AI and pathfinding

### CreateNavMesh

`CreateNavMesh(entity%, [max_slope#=45])` → integer

**(ext)** Makes a navigation mesh (the ground characters can walk on) from entity's mesh, in world space, keeping triangles that slope at most max_slope degrees; returns its handle. Use a mesh made for walking, or a level's floor. Also called CreateNavigationMesh.

```basic
; Make walkable ground from a level mesh, ignoring slopes steeper than 40 degrees
level = LoadMesh("level.b3d")
navmesh = CreateNavMesh(level, 40)
Print "Triangles: " + NavMeshTriangles(navmesh)
```

### GenerateNavMesh

`GenerateNavMesh(x#, z#, width#, depth#, [cell_size#=1], [max_slope#=45], [step_height#=0.5], [top#=1000])` → integer

**(ext)** Makes a navigation mesh by looking down on the pickable entities (EntityPickMode) over the area from x, z to x + width, z + depth every cell_size units: ground that slopes at most max_slope degrees is kept, and neighbouring points join when their heights differ by at most step_height, so walls and boxes become holes. top is the height to look down from. Returns its handle.

```basic
; Scan a 40 x 40 area every 1 unit, from a height of 50 looking down
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2              ; the scan only sees pickable entities
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40, 1, 45, 0.5, 50)
```

### NavMeshEntity

`NavMeshEntity(navmesh%)` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a mesh entity showing navmesh (just above it) and returns it, for checking what is walkable; colour it with EntityColor and EntityAlpha.

```basic
; Show the walkable area as a see-through green layer
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
view = NavMeshEntity(navmesh)
EntityColor view, 0, 255, 0
EntityAlpha view, 0.4
```

### NavMeshTriangles

`NavMeshTriangles(navmesh%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many triangles make up navmesh, the walkable surface made by GenerateNavMesh or CreateNavMesh. A very small number means little ground was found to walk on.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-10, -10, 20, 20)
Print "The navigation mesh has " + NavMeshTriangles(navmesh) + " triangles"
```

### NavMeshX

`NavMeshX(navmesh%, x#, y#, z#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the X of the point on navmesh closest to x, y, z (use with NavMeshY and NavMeshZ to keep something on walkable ground).

```basic
; Snap a point to the closest walkable ground
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-10, -10, 20, 20)
x = NavMeshX(navmesh, 3, 5, 3)
y = NavMeshY(navmesh, 3, 5, 3)
z = NavMeshZ(navmesh, 3, 5, 3)
Print "Closest walkable point: " + x + ", " + y + ", " + z
```

### NavMeshY

`NavMeshY(navmesh%, x#, y#, z#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Y of the point on navmesh closest to x, y, z.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-10, -10, 20, 20)
groundHeight = NavMeshY(navmesh, 2, 10, 2)   ; the ground height below the point 2,10,2
Print "Ground height: " + groundHeight
```

### NavMeshZ

`NavMeshZ(navmesh%, x#, y#, z#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Z of the point on navmesh closest to x, y, z.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-10, -10, 20, 20)
nearestZ = NavMeshZ(navmesh, 0, 0, 50)       ; a point far outside the mesh
Print "Nearest walkable Z: " + nearestZ
```

### FreeNavMesh

`FreeNavMesh navmesh%`

**(ext)** Frees navmesh and also frees every agent that walks on it (their entities stay). Paths already returned by FindPath are separate and stay valid until FreePath.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-10, -10, 20, 20)
FreeNavMesh navmesh                  ; also frees agents that use it
```

### CreateNavLink

`CreateNavLink(navmesh%, x1#, y1#, z1#, x2#, y2#, z2#, [two_way%=1], [cost#=1], [jump_height#=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Joins two places on navmesh that walking cannot connect, for a jump, a ladder or a teleporter pad, and returns a link number for that navmesh. Each end moves to the closest point on the navmesh. two_way 0 only goes from the first place to the second. cost multiplies the link's length when routes are compared, so a higher cost makes it a last resort. Agents cross in a straight line, or in an arc jump_height high. FindPath and agents use it by themselves.

```basic
; A jump across a gap, 3 units high at the top
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
jump = CreateNavLink(navmesh, -6, 0, 0, 6, 0, 0, 1, 1, 3)
path = FindPath(navmesh, -10, 0, 0, 10, 0, 0)
```

### NavLinkEnable

`NavLinkEnable navmesh%, link%, [enable%=1]`

**(ext)** Opens or closes link on navmesh (enable 0 is a shut door). Agents find new routes at once; one already crossing finishes.

```basic
; A door that agents stop using while it is shut
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
door = CreateNavLink(navmesh, -2, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0)
NavLinkEnable navmesh, door, 0
NavLinkEnable navmesh, door          ; open again
```

### FreeNavLink

`FreeNavLink navmesh%, link%`

**(ext)** Removes link from navmesh. The numbers of other links do not change.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
ladder = CreateNavLink(navmesh, -2, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0)
FreeNavLink navmesh, ladder
```

### FindPath

`FindPath(navmesh%, x1#, y1#, z1#, x2#, y2#, z2#)` → integer

**(ext)** Finds the shortest way over navmesh from x1, y1, z1 to x2, y2, z2 (A* with corners cut where the way is clear), using any open links, and returns a path handle, or 0 if there is no way. Read it with PathPoints and PathX/Y/Z.

```basic
; Find a route over the ground and print how many corners it has
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
path = FindPath(navmesh, -10, 0, -10, 10, 0, 10)
If path = 0 Then Print "No way there" Else Print "The path has " + PathPoints(path) + " points"
```

### PathPoints

`PathPoints(path%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many points path has (from FindPath, FindGridPath or AgentPath).

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
path = FindPath(navmesh, -10, 0, -10, 10, 0, 10)
For index = 0 To PathPoints(path) - 1
    Print "Point " + index
Next
```

### PathX

`PathX(path%, index%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the X of point index (from 0) of path; for a grid path, the column.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
path = FindPath(navmesh, -10, 0, -10, 10, 0, 10)
Print "Start X: " + PathX(path, 0)       ; points are numbered from 0
```

### PathY

`PathY(path%, index%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Y of point index (from 0) of path; for a grid path, the row.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
path = FindPath(navmesh, -10, 0, -10, 10, 0, 10)
Print "Start height: " + PathY(path, 0)
```

### PathZ

`PathZ(path%, index%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the Z of point index (from 0) of path.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
path = FindPath(navmesh, -10, 0, -10, 10, 0, 10)
lastPoint = PathPoints(path) - 1
Print "The path ends at Z " + PathZ(path, lastPoint)
```

### PathLength

`PathLength(path%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the total length of path, adding up the straight lines between its points, in world units (cells for a FindGridPath path). Use it to compare routes or estimate travel time.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
path = FindPath(navmesh, -10, 0, -10, 10, 0, 10)
Print "The walk is " + PathLength(path) + " units long"
```

### FreePath

`FreePath path%`

**(ext)** Frees path, a handle from FindPath, FindGridPath or AgentPath. Paths are not freed automatically, so free each one once you have read its points, especially if you plan routes often.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
path = FindPath(navmesh, -10, 0, -10, 10, 0, 10)
FreePath path                        ; done with it
```

### CreatePathGrid

`CreatePathGrid(width%, height%)` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a grid of width x height cells for A* pathfinding (2D games, strategy maps) and returns its handle. Every cell starts with cost 1.

```basic
; a 5 x 5 map with a wall across column 2, then a route around it
grid = CreatePathGrid(5, 5)
For row = 0 To 3
    SetPathCost grid, 2, row, 0              ; 0 = wall
Next
path = FindGridPath(grid, 0, 0, 4, 0, 0)     ; no diagonal steps
Print "Steps: " + (PathPoints(path) - 1)     ; 12, down to row 4, across, and back up
```

### SetPathCost

`SetPathCost grid%, x%, y%, cost#`

**(ext)** Sets how hard cell x, y of grid is to cross: 1 normal, higher numbers slower (mud, forest), 0 blocked.

```basic
grid = CreatePathGrid(20, 15)
SetPathCost grid, 5, 5, 0            ; 0 = blocked (a wall)
SetPathCost grid, 6, 5, 3            ; 3 = slow ground (mud)
```

### PathCost

`PathCost(grid%, x%, y%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the cost of cell x, y of grid (0 = blocked).

```basic
grid = CreatePathGrid(20, 15)
SetPathCost grid, 5, 5, 0
If PathCost(grid, 5, 5) = 0 Then Print "Cell 5,5 is blocked"
```

### FindGridPath

`FindGridPath(grid%, x1%, y1%, x2%, y2%, [diagonal%=1])` → integer

**(ext)** Finds the cheapest way over grid from cell x1, y1 to cell x2, y2 with A* and returns a path handle (PathX = column, PathY = row), or 0 if there is no way. With diagonal 1 it also moves diagonally, but never cuts past a blocked corner.

```basic
; Walk from the top-left to the bottom-right cell, around a wall
grid = CreatePathGrid(20, 15)
For row = 0 To 10
    SetPathCost grid, 10, row, 0
Next
path = FindGridPath(grid, 0, 0, 19, 14, 1)   ; 1 = diagonal steps allowed
If path <> 0 Then Print "Steps: " + PathPoints(path) + ", first column: " + PathX(path, 0)
```

### TileMapPathGrid

`TileMapPathGrid(map%)` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a path grid the size of tile map map: empty cells cost 1, cells with a tile are blocked (the same tiles TileMapBody2D makes solid). Returns its handle.

```basic
; Turn a tile map into a path grid: cells with tiles are walls
tiles = LoadAnimImage("examples/media/checker.png", 32, 32, 0, 2)
map = CreateTileMap(tiles, 20, 15)
SetTile map, 5, 5, 1
grid = TileMapPathGrid(map)
Print "Cost of the wall cell: " + PathCost(grid, 5, 5)
```

### FreePathGrid

`FreePathGrid grid%`

**(ext)** Frees grid and its cell costs. Paths already found with FindGridPath on it stay valid until you free them with FreePath.

```basic
; plan a route, then free the grid and keep the route
grid = CreatePathGrid(8, 8)
path = FindGridPath(grid, 0, 0, 7, 0)
FreePathGrid grid                  ; the grid is gone, the path is not
Print "Route has " + PathPoints(path) + " points"   ; 8
FreePath path
```

### CreateAgent

`CreateAgent(entity%, navmesh%, [speed#=3], [radius#=0.5])` → integer

**(ext)** Makes entity (a top-level entity) an agent that walks over navmesh by itself at speed units a second, keeping radius away from other agents; returns its handle. Agents move in UpdateWorld, turn to face where they go, and keep their height above the navigation mesh.

```basic
; A guard that walks over the navigation mesh by itself
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh, 4, 0.5)   ; speed 4 units a second, radius 0.5
```

### AgentMoveTo

`AgentMoveTo agent%, x#, y#, z#`

**(ext)** Sends agent to x, y, z: it finds the way and walks there. AgentArrived tells when it is there.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
AgentMoveTo agent, 10, 0, 10
While AgentArrived(agent) = 0
    UpdateWorld
Wend
```

### AgentFollow

`AgentFollow agent%, entity%, [distance#=1]`

**(ext)** Makes agent keep walking towards entity, stopping when within distance, and finding a new way as the entity moves. entity 0 stops following.

```basic
; A pet that follows the player and stops 2 units away
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
player = CreateCube()
pet = CreateSphere()
agent = CreateAgent(pet, navmesh)
AgentFollow agent, player, 2
AgentFollow agent, 0                 ; entity 0 stops following
```

### AgentStop

`AgentStop agent%`

**(ext)** Stops agent where it is: it forgets its destination, any entity it was following and its route, and AgentArrived becomes 1. Its entity stays put and can be sent off again with AgentMoveTo.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
AgentMoveTo agent, 10, 0, 10
AgentStop agent                      ; stand still where it is
```

### AgentArrived

`AgentArrived(agent%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 when agent has reached where it was sent (or is close enough to what it follows) or is not going anywhere, 0 while it is walking.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
AgentMoveTo agent, 10, 0, 10
UpdateWorld
If AgentArrived(agent) Then Print "Arrived" Else Print "Still walking"
```

### AgentOnLink

`AgentOnLink(agent%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the number of the link agent is crossing, or 0 if it is walking. Use it to play a jump or climb animation.

```basic
; Play a jump animation while an agent crosses a link
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
AgentMoveTo agent, 10, 0, 10
UpdateWorld
If AgentOnLink(agent) Then Print "In the air"
```

### AgentCrowd

`AgentCrowd agent%, [enable%=1], [look_ahead#=2]`

**(ext)** Turns crowd steering on (enable 1) or off for agent. It looks look_ahead seconds ahead and picks a direction and speed that miss other agents, so crowds flow past each other rather than bumping and being pushed apart. Agents that are close enough to their goal but blocked by others standing on it count as arrived.

```basic
; Guards that walk around each other instead of bumping
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
AgentCrowd agent                     ; looks 2 seconds ahead
AgentCrowd agent, 1, 4               ; looks further, for slow walkers
AgentCrowd agent, 0                  ; back to being pushed apart
```

### AgentDistance

`AgentDistance(agent%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns how far agent still has to walk along its path.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
AgentMoveTo agent, 10, 0, 10
UpdateWorld
Print "Still " + AgentDistance(agent) + " units to walk"
```

### AgentSpeed

`AgentSpeed agent%, speed#`

**(ext)** Sets how fast agent walks, in world units per second. Negative values count as 0, which makes the agent stand still. CreateAgent sets the starting speed.

```basic
; Make the guard run
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
AgentSpeed agent, 8                  ; units a second
```

### AgentTurnSpeed

`AgentTurnSpeed agent%, degrees#`

**(ext)** Sets how fast agent turns to face where it goes, in degrees a second (default 360; 0 does not turn it).

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
tank = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(tank, navmesh)
AgentTurnSpeed agent, 90             ; turns slowly, 90 degrees a second
```

### AgentRadius

`AgentRadius agent%, radius#`

**(ext)** Sets the size of agent in world units. Agents keep at least their two radii apart, so a larger radius gives crowds more room. CreateAgent starts it at 0.5.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
giant = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(giant, navmesh)
AgentRadius agent, 2                 ; keeps 2 units away from other agents
```

### AgentEntity

`AgentEntity(agent%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the entity that agent moves, the one passed to CreateAgent. Use it to get from an agent handle back to the entity for positioning or animation.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
body = AgentEntity(agent)
If body = guard Then Print "The agent moves the guard"
```

### AgentPath

`AgentPath(agent%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns a copy of the path agent is walking as a path handle (free it with FreePath), or 0 if it has none.

```basic
; Copy the route an agent is walking, then free the copy
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
AgentMoveTo agent, 10, 0, 10
route = AgentPath(agent)
If route <> 0
    Print "Route has " + PathPoints(route) + " points"
    FreePath route
EndIf
```

### FreeAgent

`FreeAgent agent%`

**(ext)** Frees agent so it no longer walks by itself. Its entity is not freed and stays where it was, ready to be moved by other commands.

```basic
floor = CreatePlane()
EntityPickMode floor, 2
navmesh = GenerateNavMesh(-20, -20, 40, 40)
guard = CreateCube()
agent = CreateAgent(guard, navmesh)
FreeAgent agent                      ; the guard stays where it is
```

### CreateBehavior

`CreateBehavior([name$=""])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates an empty behaviour tree for decision making and returns its handle; add nodes with BehaviorSelector, BehaviorSequence, BehaviorInvert, BehaviorIf and BehaviorDo (the first node without a parent is the root).

```basic
; Build a guard's brain: attack if the player is close, otherwise patrol
brain = CreateBehavior("guard")
root = BehaviorSelector(brain)
attack = BehaviorSequence(brain, root)
BehaviorIf brain, "dist < 5", attack
BehaviorDo brain, "attack", attack
BehaviorDo brain, "patrol", root
```

### LoadBehavior

`LoadBehavior(file$)` → integer

**(ext)** Loads a behaviour tree from a text file and returns its handle. Each line is a node, children indented under their parent: selector, sequence, invert, if <condition>, do <action>; a semicolon starts a comment.

```basic
; The file has one node per line, children indented under their parent
brain = LoadBehavior("guard.txt")
guard = CreateCube()
SetBehavior guard, brain
```

### BehaviorFromText

`BehaviorFromText(text$)` → integer

**(ext)** Makes a behaviour tree from text written like a LoadBehavior file (lines separated by Chr(10)) and returns its handle.

```basic
text = "selector" + Chr(10)
text = text + "  sequence" + Chr(10)
text = text + "    if dist < 5" + Chr(10)
text = text + "    do attack" + Chr(10)
text = text + "  do patrol"
brain = BehaviorFromText(text)
```

### BehaviorSelector

`BehaviorSelector(behavior%, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Adds a selector node under parent (0 = top level) to behavior and returns its node number: it tries its children in order until one succeeds or is running, so the first that applies wins.

```basic
; A selector tries its children in order; the first one that applies wins
brain = CreateBehavior()
root = BehaviorSelector(brain)
BehaviorDo brain, "idle", root
```

### BehaviorSequence

`BehaviorSequence(behavior%, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Adds a sequence node under parent to behavior and returns its node number: it runs its children in order while they succeed.

```basic
brain = CreateBehavior()
root = BehaviorSequence(brain)        ; runs its children one after the other
BehaviorDo brain, "walk to door", root
BehaviorDo brain, "open door", root
```

### BehaviorInvert

`BehaviorInvert(behavior%, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Adds an invert node under parent to behavior and returns its node number: its one child's success counts as failure and failure as success.

```basic
; "Not hungry" is the opposite of "hungry"
brain = CreateBehavior()
root = BehaviorSequence(brain)
notHungry = BehaviorInvert(brain, root)
BehaviorIf brain, "hungry", notHungry
BehaviorDo brain, "play", root
```

### BehaviorIf

`BehaviorIf(behavior%, condition$, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Adds a condition node under parent to behavior and returns its node number. The condition compares parameters set with SetBehaviorParam and numbers, like "dist < 10" or "health >= max" (< <= > >= = <>), or is just a parameter name (true when not 0).

```basic
brain = CreateBehavior()
root = BehaviorSequence(brain)
BehaviorIf brain, "health < 20", root   ; compares a parameter with a number
BehaviorDo brain, "flee", root
```

### BehaviorDo

`BehaviorDo(behavior%, action$, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Adds an action node under parent to behavior and returns its node number. The action is a name the program acts on when BehaviorAction returns it.

```basic
brain = CreateBehavior()
root = BehaviorSelector(brain)
BehaviorDo brain, "wander", root     ; BehaviorAction returns this name
```

### FreeBehavior

`FreeBehavior behavior%`

**(ext)** Frees behavior and removes it from every entity that was using it, so those entities have no behaviour until you give them another with SetBehavior.

```basic
brain = CreateBehavior()
BehaviorDo brain, "idle"
FreeBehavior brain
```

### SetBehavior

`SetBehavior entity%, behavior%`

**(ext)** Makes entity (or any number that names a character) use behavior; 0 removes it. Each character keeps its own parameters and progress.

```basic
brain = CreateBehavior()
BehaviorDo brain, "idle"
guard = CreateCube()
SetBehavior guard, brain             ; each character keeps its own parameters
SetBehavior guard, 0                 ; 0 removes the behaviour
```

### SetBehaviorParam

`SetBehaviorParam entity%, name$, value#`

**(ext)** Sets parameter name of entity's behaviour to value, for its conditions to compare (names are not case-sensitive; parameters not set are 0).

```basic
; Tell the brain how far away the player is, every frame
brain = CreateBehavior()
BehaviorDo brain, "idle"
guard = CreateCube()
player = CreateCube()
SetBehavior guard, brain
SetBehaviorParam guard, "dist", EntityDistance(guard, player)
```

### BehaviorParam

`BehaviorParam(entity%, name$)` → float

**(ext)** Returns parameter name of entity's behaviour as set by SetBehaviorParam, or 0 if it was never set. Names ignore capitals. It is an error if entity has no behaviour (see SetBehavior).

```basic
brain = CreateBehavior()
BehaviorDo brain, "idle"
guard = CreateCube()
SetBehavior guard, brain
SetBehaviorParam guard, "health", 80
Print "Health: " + BehaviorParam(guard, "health")
```

### BehaviorAction

`BehaviorAction(entity%)` → string

**(ext)** Evaluates entity's behaviour tree and returns the action to do now, or "" if none. Conditions are checked every time, so a more important branch takes over as soon as it applies. Do the action every frame until it is finished, then call BehaviorResult.

```basic
; Ask the brain what to do, then do it
```
