**(ext)** Changes the text of widget (button, label, text box, check box, window title and the text of list, tree, tab and menu items).

```basic
; Change the text on a button
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
button = GuiButton("Play")
SetGuiText button, "Resume"
```

### GuiText

`GuiText(widget%)` → string

**(ext)** Returns the text of widget: what was typed in a text box, the selected item of a list or drop-down, or the widget's label.

```basic
; Read what the player typed when they press Enter
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
nameBox = GuiTextBox("")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If KeyHit(28) Then Print "Hello, " + GuiText(nameBox)   ; 28 = Enter key
    Flip
Wend
```

### SetGuiValue

`SetGuiValue widget%, value#`

**(ext)** Sets the value of widget: a slider's position, a progress bar (0 to 1), a check box (1 ticked, 0 not), a number box, or a window (1 open, 0 closed).

```basic
; Set a slider, a progress bar and a check box from the program
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
volume = GuiSlider(0, 100, 50)
health = GuiProgress()
fullscreen = GuiCheckBox("Fullscreen")
SetGuiValue volume, 80
SetGuiValue health, 0.5             ; progress bars use 0 to 1
SetGuiValue fullscreen, 1           ; 1 = ticked
```

### GuiValue

`GuiValue(widget%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the value of widget (slider, progress, check box, number box, window open state, message box answer, selected index).

```basic
; Read a slider's position
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
speed = GuiSlider(1, 10, 5)
Print "Speed is " + GuiValue(speed)
```

### GuiClicked

`GuiClicked(widget%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many times widget was clicked since the last call (0 if not), like KeyHit.

```basic
; Count button presses since the last check
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
jump = GuiButton("Jump")
presses = 0
While Not KeyHit(1)
    presses = presses + GuiClicked(jump)
    Flip
Wend
Print "Pressed " + presses + " times"
```

### GuiChanged

`GuiChanged(widget%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many times the user changed widget since the last call (typed, ticked, moved, selected, closed or answered), like KeyHit.

```basic
; React only when the player edits a check box
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
sound = GuiCheckBox("Sound", 1)
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(sound) > 0 Then Print "Sound setting changed"
    Flip
Wend
```

### SetGuiPosition

`SetGuiPosition widget%, x#, y#`

**(ext)** Moves widget to x,y (logical pixels) inside the screen or a free panel.

```basic
; Place a button at a fixed spot (x, y in pixels) inside a free panel
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
canvas = GuiPanel(3)                ; layout 3 = free placement
button = GuiButton("Fire", canvas)
SetGuiPosition button, 100, 50
```

### SetGuiSize

`SetGuiSize widget%, width#, height#`

**(ext)** Sets widget's width and height in logical pixels; 0 makes that side as big as the content needs.

```basic
; Make a button 200 wide and 40 tall
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
button = GuiButton("Wide")
SetGuiSize button, 200, 40          ; width, height; 0 = as big as needed
```

### GuiMargin

`GuiMargin widget%, left#, top#, right#, bottom#`

**(ext)** Sets the space around widget: left, top, right and bottom in pixels.

```basic
; Leave 10 pixels around a button
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
button = GuiButton("Spaced")
GuiMargin button, 10, 10, 10, 10    ; left, top, right, bottom
```

### GuiAlign

`GuiAlign widget%, horizontal%, vertical%`

**(ext)** Aligns widget inside the space its container gives it: horizontal and vertical are 0 stretch, 1 left/top, 2 centre, 3 right/bottom.

```basic
; Centre a button horizontally, and put it at the top
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
button = GuiButton("Centred")
GuiAlign button, 2, 1               ; horizontal 2 = centre, vertical 1 = top
```

### GuiVisible

`GuiVisible widget%, visible%`

**(ext)** Shows (1) or hides (0) widget and everything in it.

```basic
; Hide a button and show it again
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
secret = GuiButton("Secret")
GuiVisible secret, 0                ; 0 = hidden
GuiVisible secret, 1                ; 1 = shown
```

### GuiEnable

`GuiEnable widget%, enabled%`

**(ext)** Enables (1) or disables (0) widget; a disabled widget is greyed out and ignores the user.

```basic
; Grey out a button until the player has enough gold
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
buy = GuiButton("Buy sword")
gold = 5
GuiEnable buy, 0                    ; 0 = disabled
If gold >= 10 Then GuiEnable buy, 1
```

### GuiFocus

`GuiFocus widget%`

**(ext)** Gives widget the keyboard focus (a text box can then be typed in at once).

```basic
; Let the player type straight away
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
nameBox = GuiTextBox("")
GuiFocus nameBox
```

### GuiTooltip

`GuiTooltip widget%, text$`

**(ext)** Shows text in a small box when the mouse rests on widget.

```basic
; Explain a button when the mouse rests on it
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
save = GuiButton("Save")
GuiTooltip save, "Saves your game to disk"
```

### GuiColor

`GuiColor widget%, red%, green%, blue%, [alpha%=255]`

**(ext)** Sets the background colour of widget (red, green, blue, alpha 0-255); buttons get lighter and darker shades for hover and press.

```basic
; A red button, and a half-transparent green one
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
danger = GuiButton("Delete")
GuiColor danger, 200, 40, 40        ; red, green, blue (alpha defaults to 255)
ghost = GuiButton("Ghost")
GuiColor ghost, 40, 200, 40, 128    ; alpha 128 = half see-through
```

### GuiTextColor

`GuiTextColor widget%, red%, green%, blue%`

**(ext)** Sets the text colour of widget (red, green, blue 0-255).

```basic
; Yellow text on a label
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
warning = GuiLabel("Low health!")
GuiTextColor warning, 255, 220, 0
```

### GuiFontSize

`GuiFontSize widget%, size#`

**(ext)** Sets the size of the text in widget, in pixels.

```basic
; Make a title bigger
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
title = GuiLabel("My Game")
GuiFontSize title, 32               ; size in pixels
```

### FreeGui

`FreeGui widget%`

**(ext)** Removes widget and the widgets inside it from the screen, and the handle of widget becomes invalid. Do not use the handles of widgets that were inside it afterwards.

```basic
; Remove a window and everything in it
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
win = GuiWindow("Temporary", 20, 20, 200, 100)
GuiLabel "Bye soon", win
FreeGui win
```

### GuiMouseX

`GuiMouseX(widget%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the mouse's x position relative to widget's left edge, in logical pixels.

```basic
; Show where the mouse is inside a custom image widget
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
canvas = GuiImage(CreateImage(200, 200))
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiClicked(canvas) > 0 Then Print "Clicked at " + GuiMouseX(canvas) + ", " + GuiMouseY(canvas)
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiMouseY

`GuiMouseY(widget%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the mouse's y position relative to widget's top edge, in logical pixels.

```basic
; Read the mouse height inside a widget (0 = its top edge)
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
canvas = GuiImage(CreateImage(200, 200))
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiClicked(canvas) > 0 Then Print "Distance from top: " + GuiMouseY(canvas)
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiUnderMouse

`GuiUnderMouse()` → integer

**(ext)** Returns 1 if the mouse is over a GUI widget, so a game can ignore clicks meant for the GUI.

```basic
; Only fire when the click was not meant for the GUI
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
menuButton = GuiButton("Menu")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If MouseHit(1) And GuiUnderMouse() = 0 Then Print "Shot fired"
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiTheme

`GuiTheme theme%`

**(ext)** Styles every GUI widget with the built-in dark (0) or light (1) theme.

```basic
; Switch every widget to the light theme
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
GuiTheme 1                          ; 0 = dark, 1 = light
GuiButton "Light button"
```

### GuiThemeColor

`GuiThemeColor name$, red%, green%, blue%, [alpha%=255]`

**(ext)** Changes one colour of the GUI theme for every widget: name is a theme brush such as Primary, Dark, Light, Text, Foreground, Highlight or BrightBlue (red, green, blue, alpha 0-255).

```basic
; Make the main accent colour orange everywhere
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
GuiThemeColor "Primary", 255, 140, 0   ; name, red, green, blue
GuiButton "Orange theme"
```

### GuiThemeFontSize

`GuiThemeFontSize size#`

**(ext)** Sets the text size of every GUI widget, in pixels.

```basic
; Bigger text for every widget
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
GuiThemeFontSize 20
GuiButton "Big text"
```

### GuiDropDown

`GuiDropDown(items$, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a drop-down list from items separated by "|" (e.g. "Low|Medium|High") in parent (0 = the screen) and returns its handle; the first item starts selected, and when the player picks one, GuiSelected/GuiValue give its index, GuiText its text and GuiChanged reports the change.

```basic
; A quality chooser; the first item starts selected
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
quality = GuiDropDown("Low|Medium|High")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(quality) > 0 Then Print "Quality: " + GuiText(quality) + " (index " + GuiValue(quality) + ")"
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiList

`GuiList(items$, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a list box showing items separated by "|" in parent (0 = the screen) and returns its handle; nothing starts selected, and a click selects an item: GuiSelected/GuiValue give its index, GuiText its text and GuiChanged reports the change.

```basic
; A list of weapons; nothing is selected at first
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
weapons = GuiList("Sword|Bow|Staff")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(weapons) > 0 Then Print "You picked " + GuiText(weapons)
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiAddItem

`GuiAddItem widget%, text$`

**(ext)** Adds an item with the given text to the end of a drop-down or list.

```basic
; Add an item to a list at runtime
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
inventory = GuiList("Sword|Shield")
GuiAddItem inventory, "Potion"      ; added at the end
```

### GuiRemoveItem

`GuiRemoveItem widget%, index%`

**(ext)** Removes the item at index (0 = first) from a drop-down or list; if it was selected, nothing is selected afterwards.

```basic
; Remove the first item (index 0)
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
inventory = GuiList("Sword|Shield|Potion")
GuiRemoveItem inventory, 0
```

### GuiClearItems

`GuiClearItems widget%`

**(ext)** Removes every item from a drop-down or list and clears the selection.

```basic
; Empty a list
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
inventory = GuiList("Sword|Shield|Potion")
GuiClearItems inventory
Print GuiCountItems(inventory)      ; 0
```

### GuiCountItems

`GuiCountItems(widget%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns how many items a widget has: entries of a drop-down or list, tabs of a tab control, top-level items of a tree or menu bar, or children of a tree or menu item.

```basic
; How many items does a list have?
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
inventory = GuiList("Sword|Shield|Potion")
Print "Items: " + GuiCountItems(inventory)   ; 3
```

### GuiItemText

`GuiItemText(widget%, index%)` → string

**(ext)** Returns the text of the item at index (0 = first) of a drop-down, list, tab control, tree, menu or menu item.

```basic
; Read the text of the last item
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
levels = GuiDropDown("Forest|Desert|Snow")
lastIndex = GuiCountItems(levels) - 1
Print GuiItemText(levels, lastIndex)     ; Snow
```

### GuiSelected

`GuiSelected(widget%)` → integer

**(ext)** Returns the selected item: an index (0 = first, -1 = none) for a drop-down, list or tab control (the active tab), or the handle of the selected tree item for a tree (0 = none).

```basic
; Which list item is chosen? -1 means none
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
weapons = GuiList("Sword|Bow|Staff")
If GuiSelected(weapons) = -1 Then Print "Nothing selected yet"
```

### SetGuiSelected

`SetGuiSelected widget%, index%`

**(ext)** Selects an item from the program: an index for a drop-down, list or tab control (-1 clears a drop-down or list), or a tree item's handle for a tree (0 clears); this does not count as a change for GuiChanged.

```basic
; Pick the second item from the program
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
weapons = GuiList("Sword|Bow|Staff")
SetGuiSelected weapons, 1           ; index 1 = Bow
Print GuiText(weapons)
```

### GuiTree

`GuiTree([parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates an empty tree view in parent (0 = the screen) and returns its handle; fill it with GuiTreeItem, and read the clicked item with GuiSelected (GuiText gives its text, GuiChanged reports a new selection).

```basic
; A tree view in a window
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
win = GuiWindow("Scene", 20, 20, 250, 250)
tree = GuiTree(win)
root = GuiTreeItem(tree, "World")
```

### GuiTreeItem

`GuiTreeItem(parent%, text$)` → integer

**(ext)** Adds an item with the given text to a tree, or under another tree item when parent is a tree item, and returns the item's own handle; GuiClicked(item) counts how often it was selected, and SetGuiText renames it.

```basic
; Build a small tree: World with Player and Enemies inside
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
tree = GuiTree()
world = GuiTreeItem(tree, "World")          ; parent is the tree: a top-level item
player = GuiTreeItem(world, "Player")       ; parent is an item: a child of it
enemies = GuiTreeItem(world, "Enemies")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiClicked(player) > 0 Then Print "Player selected"
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiExpand

`GuiExpand item%, expand%`

**(ext)** Opens (expand = 1) or closes (expand = 0) a tree item so its children are shown or hidden.

```basic
; Open a tree item to show its children
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
tree = GuiTree()
world = GuiTreeItem(tree, "World")
child = GuiTreeItem(world, "Player")
GuiExpand world, 1                  ; 1 = open, 0 = closed
```

### GuiTabs

`GuiTabs([parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates an empty tab control in parent (0 = the screen) and returns its handle; add pages with GuiTab, and read the active tab with GuiSelected/GuiValue (0 = first) and GuiChanged.

```basic
; A tab control; read which tab is active
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
tabs = GuiTabs()
video = GuiTab(tabs, "Video")
audio = GuiTab(tabs, "Audio")
Print "Active tab: " + GuiSelected(tabs)   ; 0 = the first
```

### GuiTab

`GuiTab(tabs%, title$)` → integer

**(ext)** Adds a page with the given title to a tab control and returns the page's handle, which can be used as the parent of other widgets (they are stacked in a column); the first page added becomes active, and SetGuiText changes the title.

```basic
; Add widgets to tab pages
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
tabs = GuiTabs()
video = GuiTab(tabs, "Video")
audio = GuiTab(tabs, "Audio")
GuiCheckBox "Fullscreen", 0, video
GuiSlider 0, 100, 80, audio
```

### GuiMenu

`GuiMenu([parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates an empty menu bar in parent (0 = the screen) and returns its handle; add its menus with GuiMenuItem.

```basic
; A menu bar across the top
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
bar = GuiMenu()
fileMenu = GuiMenuItem(bar, "File")
```

### GuiMenuItem

`GuiMenuItem(parent%, text$)` → integer

**(ext)** Adds an item with the given text to a menu bar, or to the sub-menu of another menu item when parent is a menu item, and returns the item's handle; GuiClicked(item) returns how many times it was chosen since last asked, and SetGuiText renames it.

```basic
; File menu with a Quit entry
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
bar = GuiMenu()
fileMenu = GuiMenuItem(bar, "File")
quitItem = GuiMenuItem(fileMenu, "Quit")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiClicked(quitItem) > 0 Then End
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiBorder

`GuiBorder([thickness#=1], [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a box with a border line (thickness in pixels, default 1) that holds other widgets stacked in a column; parent is the widget to put it in (0 = the screen). Returns the border's handle, which you pass as the parent of the widgets that go inside.

```basic
; A box with a 2 pixel border around two labels
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
box = GuiBorder(2)                  ; thickness, then parent
GuiLabel "Inside", box
GuiLabel "the border", box
```

### GuiPopup

`GuiPopup(width#, height#, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a hidden popup of the given width and height (0 = as big as its contents) that holds other widgets stacked in a column. Open it with GuiShowPopup; it closes when the user clicks outside it or presses Escape. GuiValue is 1 while open and 0 when closed, and GuiChanged counts the times the user closed it. Returns the popup's handle.

```basic
; A popup that opens when a button is pressed
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
options = GuiPopup(150, 80)         ; width, height
GuiLabel "Pick something", options
openButton = GuiButton("Options")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiClicked(openButton) > 0 Then GuiShowPopup options, 1
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiShowPopup

`GuiShowPopup popup%, show%`

**(ext)** Opens a popup at the mouse position (show = 1) or closes it (show = 0).

```basic
; Open a popup at the mouse, then close it again
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
tip = GuiPopup(0, 0)                ; 0 = as big as its contents
GuiLabel "Hello", tip
GuiShowPopup tip, 1                 ; 1 = open
GuiShowPopup tip, 0                 ; 0 = close
```

### GuiMessageBox

`GuiMessageBox(title$, text$, [buttons%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Shows a message box with a title and text in the middle of the screen at once and blocks clicks on the rest of the GUI until it is answered. buttons: 0 OK, 1 Yes/No, 2 Yes/No/Cancel. When the user answers, GuiChanged becomes non-zero and GuiValue is 1 for OK/Yes, 2 for No, 3 for Cancel. Returns the message box's handle.

```basic
; Ask a yes/no question
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
box = GuiMessageBox("Quit", "Really quit the game?", 1)   ; 1 = Yes/No
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(box) > 0 Then
        If GuiValue(box) = 1 Then Print "Yes" Else Print "No"   ; 1 = Yes, 2 = No
        Exit
    EndIf
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiFileBrowser

`GuiFileBrowser(path$, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a folder tree that starts at path ("" = the current folder), 300 by 250 pixels; parent is the widget to put it in (0 = the screen). When the user picks a file or folder, GuiChanged becomes non-zero and GuiText returns its path. Returns the browser's handle.

```basic
; A folder tree starting in the current folder
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
browser = GuiFileBrowser("")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(browser) > 0 Then Print "Picked " + GuiText(browser)
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiFileDialog

`GuiFileDialog(title$, path$, [save%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Opens a file dialog window in the middle of the screen at once, with a title and a starting path (a folder, or a file whose name is filled in). save = 1 makes it a Save dialog. When the user clicks Open/Save, GuiChanged becomes non-zero, GuiValue is 1 and GuiText is the chosen path; when the user clicks Cancel, GuiChanged becomes non-zero and GuiValue is 0. Returns the dialog's handle.

```basic
; An Open dialog; GuiValue is 1 for Open and 0 for Cancel
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
dialog = GuiFileDialog("Open level", "levels")
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(dialog) > 0 Then
        If GuiValue(dialog) = 1 Then Print "Open " + GuiText(dialog) Else Print "Cancelled"
        Exit
    EndIf
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiDock

`GuiDock([parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a docking area, 400 by 300 pixels (resize it with SetGuiSize), where GuiWindows can be docked side by side or as tabs and dragged in and out by the user; parent is the widget to put it in (0 = the screen). Returns the dock's handle.

```basic
; A docking area on the screen
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
dock = GuiDock()
SetGuiSize dock, 600, 400
```

### GuiDockWindow

`GuiDockWindow dock%, window%, [place%=0]`

**(ext)** Puts a GuiWindow into a dock. place: 0 leaves it floating so the user can drag it onto the dock, 1 docks it on the left, 2 right, 3 top, 4 bottom, 5 as a tab in the middle.

```basic
; Dock one window on the left and one as a tab
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
dock = GuiDock()
SetGuiSize dock, 600, 400
tools = GuiWindow("Tools", 0, 0, 150, 150)
help = GuiWindow("Help", 0, 0, 150, 150)
GuiDockWindow dock, tools, 1        ; 1 = left
GuiDockWindow dock, help, 5         ; 5 = as a tab in the middle
```

### GuiModal

`GuiModal window%`

**(ext)** Opens a GuiWindow in the middle of the screen as a modal window: the rest of the GUI ignores clicks until the window is closed.

```basic
; Open a window that blocks the rest of the GUI
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
about = GuiWindow("About", 0, 0, 250, 100)
GuiLabel "My Game v1.0", about
GuiModal about
```

### GuiCenter

`GuiCenter widget%`

**(ext)** Moves a widget to the middle of the widget it is in (the screen for widgets with parent 0).

```basic
; Centre a window on the screen
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
win = GuiWindow("Centred", 0, 0, 300, 150)
GuiCenter win
```

### GuiNumber

`GuiNumber(value#, [min#=-1000000], [max#=1000000], [step#=1], [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a number box with up/down arrows (you can also type in it or drag the arrows) showing value, kept between min and max and changed by step per click; returns the widget handle. Read it with GuiValue, set it with SetGuiValue, and GuiChanged tells you when the user edits it.

```basic
; A number box from 0 to 20 that changes by 0.5
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
gravity = GuiNumber(9.8, 0, 20, 0.5)    ; value, min, max, step
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(gravity) > 0 Then Print "Gravity: " + GuiValue(gravity)
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiVector3

`GuiVector3(x#, y#, z#, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates an editor with three number boxes for an X, Y, Z vector, starting at x, y, z; returns the widget handle. Read the parts with GuiVectorValue, set them with SetGuiVector; GuiChanged tells you when the user edits it.

```basic
; An editor for a position
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
position = GuiVector3(0, 1, 5)
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(position) > 0 Then Print "z is " + GuiVectorValue(position, 2)
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiColorPicker

`GuiColorPicker([red%=255], [green%=255], [blue%=255], [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a full colour picker (colour square, hue and alpha bars, RGB/HSV boxes) starting at the colour red, green, blue (0-255, default white); returns the widget handle. Read the colour with GuiVectorValue (0 red, 1 green, 2 blue, 3 alpha), set it with SetGuiVector; GuiChanged tells you when the user picks a colour.

```basic
; A picker that starts orange
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
picker = GuiColorPicker(255, 140, 0)
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(picker) > 0 Then Print "Red: " + GuiVectorValue(picker, 0)
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiColorField

`GuiColorField(red%, green%, blue%, [parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a small colour swatch showing red, green, blue (0-255) that opens a colour picker when clicked; returns the widget handle. The new colour arrives when the picker closes: read it with GuiVectorValue (0 red, 1 green, 2 blue, 3 alpha), set it with SetGuiVector; GuiChanged tells you when it changes.

```basic
; A colour swatch that opens a picker when clicked
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
swatch = GuiColorField(0, 128, 255)
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(swatch) > 0 Then Print "Blue: " + GuiVectorValue(swatch, 2)
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiVectorValue

`GuiVectorValue(widget%, component%)` → float

**(ext)** Returns one part of a vector editor or colour widget as a float: component 0, 1, 2 are x, y, z of a GuiVector3, or 0-3 are red, green, blue, alpha (0-255) of a GuiColorPicker or GuiColorField.

```basic
; Read one part of a vector editor
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
position = GuiVector3(1, 2, 3)
Print GuiVectorValue(position, 0)   ; 0 = x
Print GuiVectorValue(position, 1)   ; 1 = y
Print GuiVectorValue(position, 2)   ; 2 = z
```

### SetGuiVector

`SetGuiVector widget%, x#, y#, z#, [w#=255]`

**(ext)** Sets a GuiVector3 to x, y, z, or a GuiColorPicker or GuiColorField to the colour x=red, y=green, z=blue, w=alpha (0-255, 255 if left out). Does not count as a change for GuiChanged.

```basic
; Set an editor's values, and a colour picker's colour
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
position = GuiVector3(0, 0, 0)
SetGuiVector position, 5, 1, -3
picker = GuiColorPicker()
SetGuiVector picker, 255, 0, 0, 128     ; red, green, blue, alpha
```

### GuiCurve

`GuiCurve([parent%=0])` → integer

**(ext)** Creates a curve editor showing a curve that starts as a straight line from (0,0) to (1,1); the user can drag, add and remove keys (right-click for the menu). Returns the widget handle; GuiChanged tells you when the user edits the curve.

```basic
; A curve editor
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
curve = GuiCurve()
While Not KeyHit(1)
    If GuiChanged(curve) > 0 Then Print "Halfway value: " + GuiCurveValue(curve, 0.5)
    Flip
Wend
```

### GuiCurveKey

`GuiCurveKey curve%, x#, y#`

**(ext)** Adds a key (point) at position x with value y to a GuiCurve; the curve is a straight line between keys.

```basic
; A curve that rises, then falls back
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
curve = GuiCurve()
GuiClearCurve curve
GuiCurveKey curve, 0, 0             ; position x, value y
GuiCurveKey curve, 0.5, 1
GuiCurveKey curve, 1, 0
```

### GuiClearCurve

`GuiClearCurve curve%`

**(ext)** Removes every key from a GuiCurve, leaving it empty (an empty curve has the value 0 everywhere).

```basic
; Remove all keys, then add a flat line
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
curve = GuiCurve()
GuiClearCurve curve                 ; empty: the value is 0 everywhere
GuiCurveKey curve, 0, 0.5
GuiCurveKey curve, 1, 0.5
```

### GuiCurveValue

`GuiCurveValue(curve%, x#)` → float

**(ext)** Returns the value of a GuiCurve at position x, following the curve as currently edited by the user; before the first key it is the first key's value and after the last key the last key's value.

```basic
; Sample a curve, for example to ease a movement
Graphics3D 800, 600, 0, 2
curve = GuiCurve()                  ; starts as a line from (0,0) to (1,1)
Print GuiCurveValue(curve, 0.25)    ; 0.25
```

## Modern names

These names do the same as the classic command they stand for (both can be used).

| Modern name | Same as |
|---|---|
| ClearScreen | [Cls](#cls) |
| CopyModel | [CopyEntity](#copyentity) |
| CreateNavigationMesh | [CreateNavMesh](#createnavmesh) |
| DrawLine | [Line](#line) |
| DrawOval | [Oval](#oval) |
| DrawPixel | [Plot](#plot) |
| DrawRect | [Rect](#rect) |
| DrawText | [Text](#text) |
| GetEntityDistance | [EntityDistance](#entitydistance) |
| GetEntityName | [EntityName](#entityname) |
| GetEntityPitch | [EntityPitch](#entitypitch) |
| GetEntityRoll | [EntityRoll](#entityroll) |
| GetEntityX | [EntityX](#entityx) |
| GetEntityY | [EntityY](#entityy) |

| GetEntityZ | [EntityZ](#entityz) |
| JoyWait | [WaitJoy](#waitjoy) |

| LoadModel | [LoadMesh](#loadmesh) |
| LoadScene | [LoadMesh](#loadmesh) |
| LoadSceneAsync | [LoadMeshAsync](#loadmeshasync) |

| SetCameraViewport | [CameraViewport](#cameraviewport) |

| SetCollisionBox | [EntityBox](#entitybox) |
